{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfPvE4FTXYLYCSoHXTyhG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q38qk7vLGoYotTbpRo39bG","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-15efffee039b/apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations/1802200000000-DropPayrollCurrencyDefault.ts"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":55616,"cache_read_input_tokens":3309,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":55616},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2d4911eb-95e3-440f-a1b5-9a64549ec2ab","uuid":"fa26c8a0-4641-4356-8191-16e9b83d557c","timestamp":"2026-09-25T09:26:48.084Z","request_id":"req_011CfPvE3aX3TaEjAoGeQrEs","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_01Q38qk7vLGoYotTbpRo39bG":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-15efffee039b/apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations/1802200000000-DropPayrollCurrencyDefault.ts"}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q38qk7vLGoYotTbpRo39bG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * DropPayrollCurrencyDefault — remove the last hardcoded currency literal on\n5\t * the payroll write path (HR-HIGH-008).\n6\t *\n7\t * `payrolls.currency` carried `DEFAULT 'USD'` from the Baseline. The platform\n8\t * default is NOK (`HR_PLATFORM_DEFAULT_CURRENCY`) and the real answer is\n9\t * per-tenant, projected from the farm `finance_settings` SSoT into\n10\t * `hr_payroll_cost_settings`. A column default cannot know the tenant, so\n11\t * whatever it holds is wrong for someone; the only correct default is none.\n12\t *\n13\t * `CreatePayrollHandler` is the single writer of this table and now resolves\n14\t * the currency through `PayrollCostSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx`\n15\t * before the INSERT, so nothing relies on the default any more. Dropping it\n16\t * makes a currency-less payroll row structurally impossible (the column stays\n17\t * NOT NULL) rather than silently USD-denominated.\n18\t *\n19\t * Blue-green safe in both directions: the previous release also always\n20\t * supplied a currency on INSERT (it supplied the `'USD'` literal), so a\n21\t * pre-deploy pod cannot hit a NOT NULL violation against the defaultless\n22\t * column. { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\n/**\n * DropPayrollCurrencyDefault — remove the last hardcoded currency literal on\n * the payroll write path (HR-HIGH-008).\n *\n * (stack.length > 0) {\n    const dir = stack.pop();\n    if (!dir) continue;\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(dir);\n    } catch {\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (entry.startsWith('.') || entry === 'node_modules') continue;\n      const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n      try {\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WuyNM9i3suPZJoJ2AFUoLP","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Finance currency SSoT invariant (FARM-MEDIUM-145, FARM-HIGH-151, HR-HIGH-008).\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * The tenant default currency has exactly ONE source of truth:\n6\t *   - farm-service: `finance_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved through\n7\t *     `FinanceSettingsService`;\n8\t *   - hr-service: `hr_payroll_cost_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved\n9\t *     through `PayrollCostSettingsService` (itself projected from the\n10\t *     farm SSoT via the `FinanceSettingsUpdated` event).\n11\t *\n12\t * The bug this freezes: independent hardcoded ISO-currency literals drift.\n13\t * `feeds`/`equipment` defaulted to `'TRY'`, `create-feeding-record.handler.ts`\n14\t * to `'NOK'`, HR `employee.entity.ts` to `'USD'` — so a tenant recording feed\n15\t * in one form and equipment in another silently booked in different currencies.\n16\t *\n17\t * # Why this is a rule and not a list\n18\t *\n19\t * It used to guard `FINANCE_HANDLER_ROOTS` recursively PLUS a hardcoded\n20\t * `NAMED_GUARDED_FILES` array, so a currency writer outside those roots was\n21\t * covered only if somebody remembered to add it. Nobody had to: the docblock\n22\t * claimed \"there is no longer a hardcoded-currency create-handler outside this\n23\t * guarded set\" and running the spec's own pattern over every\n24\t * `apps/**\\/*.handler.ts` found SEVEN — two hr payroll handlers (HR-HIGH-008,\n25\t * a real defect: `|| 'USD'` against a platform default of NOK, stamped onto\n26\t * the cross-service `PayrollProcessed` event) and five billing handlers.\n27\t * A ratchet whose coverage is a memo is not a ratchet.\n28\t *\n29\t * So the guard now scans EVERY handler and EVERY entity, and a currency\n30\t * literal is a failure unless it appears in {@link DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS} with a\n31\t * ledger finding behind it. The exemption list can only shrink: a stale entry\n32\t * (declared but no longer matching) fails, so removing the literal forces\n33\t * removing the entry rather than leaving a permanent hole.\n34\t *\n35\t * # When this spec fails\n36\t *\n37\t *   1. A handler or entity grew a `|| 'XXX'` / `?? 'XXX'` / `currency: 'XXX'`\n38\t *      / `default: 'XXX'` currency literal. Resolve it through\n39\t *      `FinanceSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` /\n40\t *      `PayrollCostSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` instead. For a\n41\t *      column default there is no correct literal at all — a default cannot\n42\t *      know the tenant — so drop it and let the NOT NULL column force the\n43\t *      writer to supply one (precedent: migration 1802200000000).\n44\t *   2. A declared exemption no longer matches. Delete the entry; the debt it\n45\t *      recorded is paid.\n46\t *   3. An exemption cites a finding the ledger has RESOLVED. The finding says\n47\t *      the work is done, so the exemption must go with it.\n48\t */\n49\timport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n50\timport path from 'node:path';\n51\t\n52\tconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n53\tconst REGISTRY_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs/reviews/_registry/findings.jsonl');\n54\t\n55\t/**\n56\t * A recognised ISO 4217 currency code hardcoded on a currency-writing path:\n57\t *   - a fallback:       `|| 'USD'` / `?? \"nok\"`\n58\t *   - a direct assign:  `currency: 'TRY'`\n59\t *   - a column default: `default: 'USD'`\n60\t * The allowlist keeps the match precise (a bare `[A-Za-z]{3}` would also flag\n61\t * `'ASC'` sort orders and `'UTC'` zones).\n62\t */\n63\tconst CURRENCY_LITERAL =\n64\t  /(?:(?:\\|\\||\\?\\?)\\s*|currency:\\s*|default:\\s*)['\"](TRY|NOK|USD|EUR|GBP|SEK|DKK|CHF|JPY|CNY)['\"]/gi;\n65\t\n66\t/**\n67\t * Currency literals that are allowed to remain, each with the finding that\n68\t * tracks paying them off. The defect is that nothing says which reading is meant,\n79\t  // so no gate can enforce either. The fix is to drop the default\n108\t  // (a default cannot know the tenant) as migration 1802200000000 did for\n109\t  // payrolls.currency — 16 migrations across 4 services plus a NOT NULL audit,\n110\t  // which is why it is tracked rather than done inline.\n111\t  ...[\n112\t    'apps/farm-service/src/chemical/entities/chemical.entity.ts',\n113\t    'apps/farm-service/src/consumable/entities/consumable.entity.ts',\n114\t    'apps/farm-service/src/equipment/entities/equipment.entity.ts',\n115\t    'apps/farm-service/src/feed/entities/feed.entity.ts',\n116\t    'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/spare-part.entity.ts',\n117\t    'apps/farm-service/src/storage/entities/purchase-order.entity.ts',\n118\t    'apps/farm-service/src/worker/entities/worker.entity.ts',\n119\t    'apps/hr-service/src/hr/entities/employee.entity.ts',\n120\t  ].map((file) => ({\n121\t    file,\n122\t    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n123\t    reason: 'entity currency column still carries a literal DDL default',\n124\t  })),\n125\t\n126\t  // FARM-MEDIUM-327, but the LIVE member of the set and the one this rule\n127\t  // found that a handler-only scan could not: `work_orders.currency` is\n128\t  // nullable with no default, while the derived `costSummary` jsonb requires a\n129\t  // non-null `currency`, so `updateCostSummary()` stamps TRY on every work\n130\t  // order created without one — whatever the tenant's currency is. An entity\n131\t  // method cannot reach FinanceSettingsService, so the fix is to derive the\n132\t  // cost summary in the handler that can, not to swap the literal.\n133\t  {\n134\t    file: 'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/work-order.entity.ts',\n135\t    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n136\t    reason: 'costSummary derives a non-null currency from a nullable column inside the entity',\n137\t  },\n138\t];\n139\t\n140\tconst EXEMPT_FILES = new Set(DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.map((entry) => entry.file));\n141\t\n142\t/** Every `*.handler.ts` and `*.entity.ts` under `apps/`, tests excluded. */\n143\tfunction listCurrencyWritingFiles(): string[] {\n144\t  const out: string[] = [];\n145\t  const walk = (current: string): void => {\n146\t    let entries: string[];\n147\t    try {\n148\t      entries = readdirSync(current);\n149\t    } catch {\n150\t      return;\n151\t    }\n152\t    for (const entry of entries) {\n153\t      const full = path.join(current, entry);\n154\t      if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) {\n155\t        if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__') continue;\n156\t        walk(full);\n157\t        continue;\n158\t      }\n159\t      if (entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) continue;\n160\t      if (/\\.(handler|entity)\\.ts$/.test(entry)) out.push(full);\n161\t    }\n162\t  };\n163\t  walk(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps'));\n164\t  return out;\n165\t}\n166\t\n167\tfunction currencyLiteralsIn(file: string): string[] {\n168\t  const matches = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').match(CURRENCY_LITERAL);\n169\t  return matches ? [...new Set(matches)] : [];\n170\t}\n171\t\n172\tfunction resolvedFindingIds(): Set<string> {\n173\t  const resolved = new Set<string>();\n174\t  for (const line of readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, === 'RESOLVED') resolved.add(entry.id);\n178\t  }\n179\t  return resolved;\n180\t}\n181\t\n182\tdescribe('Finance currency SSoT invariant', () => {\n183\t  const files = {\n206\t    const stale = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => !offenders.has(entry.file)).map(\n207\t      (entry) => `${entry.file} (${entry.finding}) — no currency literal left; delete this entry`,\n208\t    );\n209\t\n210\t    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n211\t  });\n212\t\n213\t  it('every exemption cites a finding the ledger still has open', () => {\n214\t    const resolved = resolvedFindingIds();\n215\t    const contradicted = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => resolved.has(entry.finding)).map(\n216\t      (entry) => `${entry.file} cites ${entry.finding}, which is RESOLVED`,\n217\t    );\n218\t\n219\t    expect(contradicted).toEqual([]);\n220\t  });\n221\t});\n222\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2d4911eb-95e3-440f-a1b5-9a64549ec2ab","uuid":"cfe4d468-16e2-4cf3-b5eb-fba821f34cb6","timestamp":"2026-09-25T09:26:51.558Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-15efffee039b/tests/invariants/finance-currency-ssot.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Finance currency SSoT invariant (FARM-MEDIUM-145, FARM-HIGH-151, HR-HIGH-008).\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * The tenant default currency has exactly ONE source of truth:\n *   - farm-service: `finance_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved through\n *     `FinanceSettingsService`;\n *   - hr-service: `hr_payroll_cost_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved\n *     through `PayrollCostSettingsService` (itself projected from the\n *     farm SSoT via the `FinanceSettingsUpdated` event).\n *\n * The bug this freezes: independent hardcoded ISO-currency literals drift.\n * `feeds`/`equipment` defaulted to `'TRY'`, `create-feeding-record.handler.ts`\n * to `'NOK'`, HR `employee.entity.ts` to `'USD'` — so a tenant recording feed\n * in one form and equipment in another silently booked in different currencies.\n *\n * # Why this is a rule and not a list\n *\n * It used to guard `FINANCE_HANDLER_ROOTS` recursively PLUS a hardcoded\n * `NAMED_GUARDED_FILES` array, so a currency writer outside those roots was\n * covered only if somebody remembered to add it. Nobody had to: the docblock\n * claimed \"there is no longer a hardcoded-currency create-handler outside this\n * guarded set\" and running the spec's own pattern over every\n * `apps/**\\/*.handler.ts` found SEVEN — two hr payroll handlers (HR-HIGH-008,\n * a real defect: `|| 'USD'` against a platform default of NOK, stamped onto\n * the cross-service `PayrollProcessed` event) and five billing handlers.\n * A ratchet whose coverage is a memo is not a ratchet.\n *\n * So the guard now scans EVERY handler and EVERY entity, and a currency\n * literal is a failure unless it appears in {@link DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS} with a\n * ledger finding behind it. The exemption list can only shrink: a stale entry\n * (declared but no longer matching) fails, so removing the literal forces\n * removing the entry rather than leaving a permanent hole.\n *\n * # When this spec fails\n *\n *   1. A handler or entity grew a `|| 'XXX'` / `?? 'XXX'` / `currency: 'XXX'`\n *      / `default: 'XXX'` currency literal. Resolve it through\n *      `FinanceSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` /\n *      `PayrollCostSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` instead. For a\n *      column default there is no correct literal at all — a default cannot\n *      know the tenant — so drop it and let the NOT NULL column force the\n *      writer to supply one (precedent: migration 1802200000000).\n *   2. A declared exemption no longer matches. Delete the entry; the debt it\n *      recorded is paid.\n *   3. An exemption cites a finding the ledger has RESOLVED. The finding says\n *      the work is done, so the exemption must go with it.\n */\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst REGISTRY_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs/reviews/_registry/findings.jsonl');\n\n/**\n * A recognised ISO 4217 currency code hardcoded on a currency-writing path:\n *   - a fallback:       `|| 'USD'` / `?? \"nok\"`\n *   - a direct assign:  `currency: 'TRY'`\n *   - a column default: `default: 'USD'`\n * The allowlist keeps the match precise (a bare `[A-Za-z]{3}` would also flag\n * `'ASC'` sort orders and `'UTC'` zones).\n */\nconst CURRENCY_LITERAL =\n  /(?:(?:\\|\\||\\?\\?)\\s*|currency:\\s*|default:\\s*)['\"](TRY|NOK|USD|EUR|GBP|SEK|DKK|CHF|JPY|CNY)['\"]/gi;\n\n/**\n * Currency literals that are allowed to remain, each with the finding that\n * tracks paying them off. NOT an exemption mechanism for new code: adding an\n * entry means registering a finding with an owner and a deadline first.\n */\nconst DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{\n  readonly file: string;\n  readonly finding: string;\n  readonly reason: string;\n}> = [\n  // BILLING-MEDIUM-016 — platform-vs-tenant currency is undeclared in billing.\n  // Not asserted to be a defect: Stripe-facing platform billing in USD is a\n  // defensible policy. The defect is that nothing says which reading is meant,\n  // so no gate can enforce either. Declaring it is the fix; until then the\n  // exemption makes the ambiguity visible instead of it being the absence of a\n  // list entry.\n  ...[\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/change-subscription-plan.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-invoice.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-plan.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-subscription.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/query-handlers/get-tenant-billing.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/custom-plan.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/discount-code.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/invoice.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/module-price.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/payment.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/plan.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/subscription-module-item.entity.ts',\n    'apps/admin-api-service/src/analytics/entities/external/invoice.entity.ts',\n  ].map((file) => ({\n    file,\n    finding: 'BILLING-MEDIUM-016',\n    reason: 'platform-vs-tenant billing currency is undeclared',\n  })),\n\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-327 — the handlers were migrated to the settings SSoT; the DDL\n  // layer they write into was not. These columns disagree with each other\n  // inside one service (TRY / NOK / USD on sibling farm tables), which is the\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-145 drift one layer down. No live write reaches them because\n  // every handler supplies a currency, but each is a loaded trap for a\n  // backfill, an admin tool or the next writer. The fix is to drop the default\n  // (a default cannot know the tenant) as migration 1802200000000 did for\n  // payrolls.currency — 16 migrations across 4 services plus a NOT NULL audit,\n  // which is why it is tracked rather than done inline.\n  ...[\n    'apps/farm-service/src/chemical/entities/chemical.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/consumable/entities/consumable.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/equipment/entities/equipment.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/feed/entities/feed.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/spare-part.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/storage/entities/purchase-order.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/worker/entities/worker.entity.ts',\n    'apps/hr-service/src/hr/entities/employee.entity.ts',\n  ].map((file) => ({\n    file,\n    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n    reason: 'entity currency column still carries a literal DDL default',\n  })),\n\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-327, but the LIVE member of the set and the one this rule\n  // found that a handler-only scan could not: `work_orders.currency` is\n  // nullable with no default, while the derived `costSummary` jsonb requires a\n  // non-null `currency`, so `updateCostSummary()` stamps TRY on every work\n  // order created without one — whatever the tenant's currency is. An entity\n  // method cannot reach FinanceSettingsService, so the fix is to derive the\n  // cost summary in the handler that can, not to swap the literal.\n  {\n    file: 'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/work-order.entity.ts',\n    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n    reason: 'costSummary derives a non-null currency from a nullable column inside the entity',\n  },\n];\n\nconst EXEMPT_FILES = new Set(DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.map((entry) => entry.file));\n\n/** Every `*.handler.ts` and `*.entity.ts` under `apps/`, tests excluded. */\nfunction listCurrencyWritingFiles(): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  const walk = (current: string): void => {\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(current);\n    } catch {\n      return;\n    }\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      const full = path.join(current, entry);\n      if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) {\n        if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__') continue;\n        walk(full);\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) continue;\n      if (/\\.(handler|entity)\\.ts$/.test(entry)) out.push(full);\n    }\n  };\n  walk(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps'));\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction currencyLiteralsIn(file: string): string[] {\n  const matches = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').match(CURRENCY_LITERAL);\n  return matches ? [...new Set(matches)] : [];\n}\n\nfunction resolvedFindingIds(): Set<string> {\n  const resolved = new Set<string>();\n  for (const line of readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, 'utf8').trim().split('\\n')) {\n    if (!line) continue;\n    const entry = JSON.parse(line) as { id?: unknown; state?: unknown };\n    if (typeof entry.id === 'string' && entry.state === 'RESOLVED') resolved.add(entry.id);\n  }\n  return resolved;\n}\n\ndescribe('Finance currency SSoT invariant', () => {\n  const files = listCurrencyWritingFiles();\n  const offenders = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const file of files) {\n    const literals = currencyLiteralsIn(file);\n    if (literals.length > 0) offenders.set(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, file), literals);\n  }\n\n  it('scans every handler and entity under apps/ (extractor sanity)', () => {\n    // A broken walker would report zero offenders and pass everything below.\n    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(500);\n    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('create-feeding-record.handler.ts'))).toBe(true);\n    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('payroll.entity.ts'))).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('no handler or entity hardcodes a currency outside a declared exemption', () => {\n    const undeclared = [...offenders]\n      .filter(([file]) => !EXEMPT_FILES.has(file))\n      .map(([file, literals]) => `${file} → ${literals.join(', ')}`);\n\n    expect(undeclared).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every declared exemption still carries a literal (the list only shrinks)', () => {\n    const stale = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => !offenders.has(entry.file)).map(\n      (entry) => `${entry.file} (${entry.finding}) — no currency literal left; delete this entry`,\n    );\n\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every exemption cites a finding the ledger still has open', () => {\n    const resolved = resolvedFindingIds();\n    const contradicted = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => resolved.has(entry.finding)).map(\n      (entry) => `${entry.file} cites ${entry.finding}, which is RESOLVED`,\n    );\n\n    expect(contradicted).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":222,"startLine":1,"totalLines":222}}}

probeErr.stack : undefined,\n        );\n        const probeErrInstance = new Error(\n          `Migration \"${migration.name}\" postCondition() threw on \"${schema}\" — ` +\n            `DDL did not satisfy declared invariant. Rolling back.`,\n        );\n        // Attach cause without depending on Error options (lib.es2022.error.d.ts);\n        // this stays compatible with older TS lib targets while preserving the chain.\n        (probeErrInstance as Error & { cause?: unknown }).cause = probeErr;\n        throw probeErrInstance;\n      }\n\n      if (result === false) {\n        this.logger.error(\n          `[postCondition] Migration \"${migration.name}\" returned false on \"${schema}\"`,\n        );\n        throw new Error(\n          `Migration \"${migration.name}\" postCondition() returned false on \"${schema}\" — ` +\n            `DDL did not satisfy declared invariant.    return null;\n  }\n\n  const rowsResult: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n    `SELECT \"timestamp\"::text AS timestamp, \"name\"\n       FROM \"${schema}\".\"${migrationsTableName}\"\n      ORDER BY \"timestamp\" DESC, \"id\" DESC\n      LIMIT 1`,\n  );\n  const row = rowsFromQueryResult(rowsResult)[0];\n  const timestamp = stringColumn(row, 'timestamp');\n  const name = stringColumn(row, 'name');\n  if (timestamp === null || name === null) return null;\n  return {\n    timestamp,\n    name,\n  };\n}\n\nasync function runPostConditionProbe(\n  migration: { name: string; instance?: unknown },\n  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n  schema: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const instance = migration.instance;\n  if (instance === null || typeof instance !== 'object') {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const candidate = instance as PostConditionAwareMigration & MigrationInterface;\n  if (typeof candidate.postCondition !== 'function') {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let result: unknown;\n  try {\n    result = await candidate.postCondition(queryRunner, schema);\n  } catch (probeErr) {\n    const wrapped = new Error(\n      `Migration \"${migration.name}\" postCondition() threw on \"${schema}\" — ` +\n        `DDL did not satisfy its declared invariant. Rolling back.`,\n    );\n    (wrapped as Error & { cause?: unknown }).cause = probeErr;\n    throw wrapped;\n  }\n\n  if (result === false) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Migration \"${migration.name}\" postCondition() returned false on \"${schema}\" — ` +\n        `DDL did not satisfy its declared invariant. Rolling back.`,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nasync function recordSourceOnlySkip(\n  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n  schema: string,\n  migrationsTableName: string,\n  migration: Migration,\n): Promise<void> {\n  await queryRunner.query(\n    `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"${schema}\".\"${migrationsTableName}\" (\n       \"id\" SERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,\n       \"timestamp\" bigint NOT NULL,\n       \"name\" varchar NOT NULL\n     )`,\n  );\n  await queryRunner.query(\n    `INSERT INTO \"${schema}\".\"${migrationsTableName}\" (\"timestamp\", \"name\")\n     SELECT $1::bigint, $2::varchar\n     WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM \"${schema}\".\"${migrationsTableName}\"\n        WHERE \"timestamp\" = $1::bigint AND \"name\" = $2::varchar\n     )`,\n    [migration.timestamp, migration.name],\n  );\n}\n\nasync function withLockedMigrationSession<T>(\n  opts: RunSchemaOptions,","numLines":80,"startLine":255,"totalLines":682}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013PxDEZSJhvAQzyK2Gk5CX9","type":"tool_result","content":"395\t      - name: Install dependencies\n396\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n397\t\n398\t      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n399\t        env:\n400\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n401\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n402\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n403\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n404\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n405\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n406\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n407\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n408\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n409\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n410\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n411\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n412\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n413\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n414\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n415\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n416\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n417\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n418\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n419\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n420\t        run: |\n421\t          npx ts-node \\\n422\t            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n423\t            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n424\t            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n425\t\n426\t      - name: Run tenant-clone-parity test\n427\t        run: npm run test:tenant-clone\n428\t        env:\n429\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n430\t\n431\t      # DATA-CRITICAL-010: the gate the finding's own closure criterion asks\n432\t      # for. Until this step, NO spec in the repository entered the PROVISION\n433\t      # branch — so \"a super admin creates a tenant\", the first thing the pilot\n434\t      # does, was the one path CI never executed, and a Baseline corpus that\n435\t      # could not be replayed into any tenant schema stayed green for four\n436\t      # months. It runs here because the database above has just been migrated\n437\t      # by the real db-migrate entry point, which is the same binary the spec\n438\t      # then invokes in `tenant-schema-provisioner --once` mode. The tenant it\n439\t      # creates is left in place on purpose: schema-invariants B.5a asserts\n440\t      # every tenant-scoped schema has at least one clone, and with\n441\t      # tenant-clone-parity dropping its fixture in afterAll that assertion was\n442\t      # passing against zero tenants on every run.\n443\t      - name: Provision a real tenant through the production provisioner\n444\t        env:\n445\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n446\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n447\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n448\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n449\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n450\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n451\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n452\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n453\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n454\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n455\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n456\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n457\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n458\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n459\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n460\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n461\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n462\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n463\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n464\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n465\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n466\t        run: npm run test:tenant-provisioning\n467\t\n468\t      # SENSOR-MEDIUM-052: CLAUDE.md named this spec as the per-PR schema\n469\t      # invariant gate, but nothing invoked it — this workflow only listed it as\n470\t      # a path filter. It runs here because the database above has just been\n471\t      # migrated by the real db-migrate entry point, which is exactly the live\n472\t      # DDL the spec asserts against (public/shared layout, per-service\n473\t      # cross-tenant `schema:` placement).\n474\t      - name: Run schema invariants against the migrated database\n475\t        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n476\t        env:\n477\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n478\t\n479\t  # ===================================================================\n480\t  # schema-invariants — physical schema layout gate (FARM-MEDIUM-303).\n481\t  #\n482\t  # `e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts` named this\n483\t  # workflow as its home since the 2026-04-14 public-schema teardown,\n484\t  # but only ever appeared in the `paths:` filters above — it triggered\n485\t  # the workflow and was then never executed by any step. A spec that\n486\t  # nothing runs is not a gate: the B.1/B.2 assertions (an @Entity that\n487\t  # forgets `schema:` lands in `public`; a declared schema that does not\n488\t  # match the physical one) were silently uncovered the whole time,\n489\t  # which is exactly the multi-tenant isolation regression the teardown\n490\t  # plan was built to prevent.\n491\t  #\n492\t  # Why its own job rather than a step on tenant-clone-parity: the two\n493\t  # specs assert different things and must attribute their failures\n494\t  # separately — \"which invariant broke\" is readable from the job name,\n495\t  # matching how every other gate in this workflow is structured.\n496\t  #\n497\t  # Preconditions the spec needs, and where they come from:\n498\t  #   - every service schema present + owned by `<svc>_service`\n499\t  #     (assertion B.4) — created by aqua-db-migrate's platform\n500\t  #     bootstrap stage 003, which the migrate step below runs;\n501\t  #   - `shared` holding exactly the canonical cross-service tables\n502\t  #     (B.3) — bootstrap stage 006.\n503\t  # B.5a (tenant clones exist) is vacuous here because a freshly\n504\t  # migrated DB has no tenants; that is by the spec's own design — the\n505\t  # non-vacuous version of that check is tenant-clone-parity above,\n506\t  # which provisions a tenant schema and asserts against it directly.\n507\t  # ===================================================================\n508\t  schema-invariants:\n509\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n510\t    timeout-minutes: 10\n511\t    env:\n512\t      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n513\t\n514\t    services:\n515\t      postgres:\n516\t        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n517\t        env:\n518\t          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n519\t          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n520\t          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n521\t        ports:\n522\t          - 5432:5432\n523\t        options: >-\n524\t          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n525\t          --health-interval 10s\n526\t          --health-timeout 5s\n527\t          --health-retries 10\n528\t\n529\t    steps:\n530\t      - name: Checkout repository\n531\t        uses: actions/checkout@9c091bb21b7c1c1d1991bb908d89e4e9dddfe3e0 # v7.0.0\n532\t\n533\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n534\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n535\t        with:\n536\t          node-version: '22'\n537\t          cache: 'npm'\n538\t\n539\t      - name: Install dependencies\n540\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n541\t\n542\t      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n543\t        env:\n544\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n545\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n546\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n547\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n548\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n549\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n550\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n551\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n552\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n553\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n554\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n555\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n556\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n557\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n558\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n559\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n560\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n561\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n562\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n563\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n564\t        run: |\n565\t          npx ts-node \\\n566\t            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n567\t            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n568\t            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n569\t\n570\t      - name: Run schema-invariants test\n571\t        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n572\t        env:\n573\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n574\t\n575\t  # ===================================================================\n576\t  # migration-deletion-witness — pre-merge gate.\n577\t  #\n578\t  # Catches the silent-regression class where a migration file (or\n579\t  # an init-script with table-creating SQL) is deleted without\n580\t  # either a sibling-service migration carrying the same CREATE\n581\t  # forward, an init-script equivalent, or an explicit\n582\t  # `DELETE-OK: <reason>` line in the PR body. name: Run tenant-clone-parity test\n        run: npm run test:tenant-clone\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n\n      # DATA-CRITICAL-010: the gate the finding's own closure criterion asks\n      # for. Until this step, NO spec in the repository entered the PROVISION\n      # branch — so \"a super admin creates a tenant\", the first thing the pilot\n      # does, was the one path CI never executed, and a Baseline corpus that\n      # could not be replayed into any tenant schema stayed green for four\n      # months. It runs here because the database above has just been migrated\n      # by the real db-migrate entry point, which is the same binary the spec\n      # then invokes in `tenant-schema-provisioner --once` mode. The tenant it\n      # creates is left in place on purpose: schema-invariants B.5a asserts\n      # every tenant-scoped schema has at least one clone, and with\n      # tenant-clone-parity dropping its fixture in afterAll that assertion was\n      # passing against zero tenants on every run.\n      - name: Provision a real tenant through the production provisioner\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n        run: npm run test:tenant-provisioning\n\n      # SENSOR-MEDIUM-052: CLAUDE.md named this spec as the per-PR schema\n      # invariant gate, but nothing invoked it — this workflow only listed it as\n      # a path filter. It runs here because the database above has just been\n      # migrated by the real db-migrate entry point, which is exactly the live\n      # DDL the spec asserts against (public/shared layout, per-service\n      # cross-tenant `schema:` placement).\n      - name: Run schema invariants against the migrated database\n        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # schema-invariants — physical schema layout gate (FARM-MEDIUM-303).\n  #\n  # `e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts` named this\n  # workflow as its home since the 2026-04-14 public-schema teardown,\n  # but only ever appeared in the `paths:` filters above — it triggered\n  # the workflow and was then never executed by any step. A spec that\n  # nothing runs is not a gate: the B.1/B.2 assertions (an @Entity that\n  # forgets `schema:` lands in `public`; a declared schema that does not\n  # match the physical one) were silently uncovered the whole time,\n  # which is exactly the multi-tenant isolation regression the teardown\n  # plan was built to prevent.\n  #\n  # Why its own job rather than a step on tenant-clone-parity: the two\n  # specs assert different things and must attribute their failures\n  # separately — \"which invariant broke\" is readable from the job name,\n  # matching how every other gate in this workflow is structured.\n  #\n  # Preconditions the spec needs, and where they come from:\n  #   - every service schema present + owned by `<svc>_service`\n  #     (assertion B.4) — created by aqua-db-migrate's platform\n  #     bootstrap stage 003, which the migrate step below runs;\n  #   - `shared` holding exactly the canonical cross-service tables\n  #     (B.3) — bootstrap stage 006.\n  # B.5a (tenant clones exist) is vacuous here because a freshly\n  # migrated DB has no tenants; that is by the spec's own design — the\n  # non-vacuous version of that check is tenant-clone-parity above,\n  # which provisions a tenant schema and asserts against it directly.\n  # ===================================================================\n  schema-invariants:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 10\n    env:\n      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n\n    services:\n      postgres:\n        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n        env:\n          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n        ports:\n          - 5432:5432\n        options: >-\n          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n          --health-interval 10s\n          --health-timeout 5s\n          --health-retries 10\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@9c091bb21b7c1c1d1991bb908d89e4e9dddfe3e0 # v7.0.0\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n        env:\n          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n        run: |\n          npx ts-node \\\n            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n\n      -  settings: { getDefaultCurrencyInTx: jest.Mock };\n  managerOf: () => EntityManager;\n} {\n  const { queryRunner, created } = buildQueryRunner(employee);\n  const dataSource = collaborator<DataSource>(\n    { createQueryRunner: jest.fn().mockReturnValue(queryRunner) },\n    'DataSource',\n  );\n  const getDefaultCurrencyInTx = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(defaultCurrency);\n  const settings = collaborator<PayrollCostSettingsService>(\n    { getDefaultCurrencyInTx },\n    'PayrollCostSettingsService',\n  );\n\n  // The handler never touches the two injected repositories on this path — it\n  // works through queryRunner.manager — so an empty collaborator is the honest\n  // double: any use would throw MissingDoubleMemberError naming the member.\n  const handler = new CreatePayrollHandler(\n    collaborator<Repository<Payroll>>({}, 'Repository<Payroll>'),\n    collaborator<Repository<Employee>>({}, 'Repository<Employee>'),\n    dataSource,\n    settings,\n  );\n\n  return {\n    handler,\n    created,\n    settings: { getDefaultCurrencyInTx },\n    managerOf: () => queryRunner.manager,\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe('CreatePayrollHandler — currency SSoT (HR-HIGH-008)', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => jest.clearAllMocks());\n\n  it('falls back to the tenant default from the settings SSoT, never to a literal', async () => {\n    const { handler, created, settings, managerOf } = buildHandler(buildEmployee());\n\n    await handler.execute(new CreatePayrollCommand(TENANT_ID, buildInput(), USER_ID));\n\n    expect(settings.getDefaultCurrencyInTx).toHaveBeenCalledWith(managerOf(), TENANT_ID);\n    expect(created).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(created[0]?.currency).toBe(TENANT_DEFAULT_CURRENCY);\n    // The literal the handler used to carry. Asserting the SSoT value alone\n    // would still pass if a `|| 'USD'` were left downstream of it.\n    expect(created[0]?.currency).not.toBe('USD');\n  });\n\n  it(\"prefers the employee's own currency over the tenant default\", async () => {\n    const { handler, created } = buildHandler(buildEmployee({ currency: 'NOK' }));\n\n    await handler.execute(new CreatePayrollCommand(TENANT_ID, buildInput(), USER_ID));\n\n    expect(created[0]?.currency).toBe('NOK');\n  });\n\n  it('prefers an explicit input currency over everything', async () => {\n    const { handler, created } = buildHandler(buildEmployee({ currency: 'NOK' }));\n\n    await handler.execute(\n      new CreatePayrollCommand(TENANT_ID, buildInput({ currency: 'EUR' }), USER_ID),\n    );\n\n    expect(created[0]?.currency).toBe('EUR');\n  });\n\n  it('resolves the default inside the handler transaction, not on a separate connection', async () => {\n    // getDefaultCurrencyInTx caches per tenant; passing the handler's own\n    // manager is what keeps the read inside the payroll transaction rather\n    // than opening a second one that could see a different snapshot.","numLines":70,"startLine":120,"totalLines":198}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01URuuLvjBaXE5xa174TbbyB","type":"tool_result","content":"300\t          `SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default\n301\t           FROM information_schema.columns\n302\t           WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2\n303\t           ORDER BY column_name`,\n304\t          [sourceSchema, table],\n305\t        );\n306\t        const cloneCols = await db.query<ColumnRow>(\n307\t          `SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default\n308\t           FROM information_schema.columns\n309\t           WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2\n310\t           ORDER BY column_name`,\n311\t          [tenantSchemaName, table],\n312\t        );\n313\t        const sourceMap = new Map(sourceCols.rows.map((c) => [c.column_name, c]));\n314\t        const cloneMap = new Map(cloneCols.rows.map((c) => [c.column_name, c]));\n315\t\n316\t        for (const [colName, src] of sourceMap) {\n317\t          const clone = cloneMap.get(colName);\n318\t          if (!clone) {\n319\t            drifts.push(`${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: missing in clone`);\n320\t            continue;\n321\t          }\n322\t          if (clone.data_type !== src.data_type) {\n323\t            drifts.push(\n324\t              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: data_type drift ` +\n325\t                `(source=${src.data_type}, clone=${clone.data_type})`,\n326\t            );\n327\t          }\n328\t          if (clone.is_nullable !== src.is_nullable) {\n329\t            drifts.push(\n330\t              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: is_nullable drift ` +\n331\t                `(source=${src.is_nullable}, clone=${clone.is_nullable})`,\n332\t            );\n333\t          }\n334\t          // column_default may legitimately differ when the source\n335\t          // uses a sequence (nextval('source_seq')); CREATE TABLE\n336\t          // LIKE INCLUDING ALL replicates the default literally and\n337\t          // the sequence reference flips to the clone's per-table\n338\t          // sequence. Treat default-string difference as a drift\n339\t          // ONLY when source has a literal default and clone has\n340\t          // none — the inverse is normal LIKE-semantics.\n341\t          if (src.column_default && !clone.column_default && !/nextval/.test(src.column_default)) {\n342\t            drifts.push(\n343\t              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: column_default drift ` +\n344\t                `(source=${src.column_default}, clone=null)`,\n345\t            );\n346\t          }\n347\t        }\n348\t        // Extra columns in clone (not in source) — the LIKE INCLUDING\n349\t        // ALL contract should never produce these. Surface as drift.\n350\t        for (const colName of cloneMap.keys()) {\n351\t          if (!sourceMap.has(colName)) {\n352\t            drifts.push(`${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: extra column not in source`);\n353\t          }\n354\t        }\n355\t      }\n356\t    }\n357\t    if (drifts.length > 0) {\n358\t      throw new Error(\n359\t        `Tenant-clone column-shape drift (${drifts.length} issue(s)):\\n  ` + drifts.join('\\n  '),\n360\t      );\n361\t    }\n362\t  });\n363\t\n364\t  it('every cloned table has RLS enabled (pg_class.relrowsecurity = true)', async () => {\n365\t    const offenders: string[] = [];\n366\t    for (const sourceSchema of TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS) {\n367\t      const tables = provisionedTables.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n368\t      for (const table of tables) {\n369\t        const result = await db.query<{ relrowsecurity: boolean }>(\n370\t          `SELECT c.relrowsecurity\n371\t           FROM pg_class c\n372\t           JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace\n373\t           WHERE n.nspname = $1 AND c.relname = $2 AND c.relkind = 'r'`,\n374\t          [tenantSchemaName, table],"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2d4911eb-95e3-440f-a1b5-9a64549ec2ab","uuid":"9e2aef90-d14d-43b0-9e1f-5b325781cda0","timestamp":"2026-09-25T09:28:38.888Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-15efffee039b/e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts","content":"          `SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default\n           FROM information_schema.columns\n           WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2\n           ORDER BY column_name`,\n          [sourceSchema, table],\n        );\n        const cloneCols = await db.query<ColumnRow>(\n          `SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default\n           FROM information_schema.columns\n           WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2\n           ORDER BY column_name`,\n          [tenantSchemaName, table],\n        );\n        const sourceMap = new Map(sourceCols.rows.map((c) => [c.column_name, c]));\n        const cloneMap = new Map(cloneCols.rows.map((c) => [c.column_name, c]));\n\n        for (const [colName, src] of sourceMap) {\n          const clone = cloneMap.get(colName);\n          if (!clone) {\n            drifts.push(`${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: missing in clone`);\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (clone.data_type !== src.data_type) {\n            drifts.push(\n              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: data_type drift ` +\n                `(source=${src.data_type}, clone=${clone.data_type})`,\n            );\n          }\n          if (clone.is_nullable !== src.is_nullable) {\n            drifts.push(\n              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: is_nullable drift ` +\n                `(source=${src.is_nullable}, clone=${clone.is_nullable})`,\n            );\n          }\n          // column_default may legitimately differ when the source\n          // uses a sequence (nextval('source_seq')); CREATE TABLE\n          // LIKE INCLUDING ALL replicates the default literally and\n          // the sequence reference flips to the clone's per-table\n          // sequence. Treat default-string difference as a drift\n          // ONLY when source has a literal default and clone has\n          // none — the inverse is normal LIKE-semantics.\n          if (src.column_default && !clone.column_default && !/nextval/.test(src.column_default)) {\n            drifts.push(\n              `${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: column_default drift ` +\n                `(source=${src.column_default}, clone=null)`,\n            );\n          }\n        }\n        // Extra columns in clone (not in source) — the LIKE INCLUDING\n        // ALL contract should never produce these. Surface as drift.\n        for (const colName of cloneMap.keys()) {\n          if (!sourceMap.has(colName)) {\n            drifts.push(`${tenantSchemaName}.${table}.${colName}: extra column not in source`);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (drifts.length > 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Tenant-clone column-shape drift (${drifts.length} issue(s)):\\n  ` + drifts.join('\\n  '),\n      );\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('every cloned table has RLS enabled (pg_class.relrowsecurity = true)', async () => {\n    const offenders: string[] = [];\n    for (const sourceSchema of TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS) {\n      const tables = provisionedTables.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n      for (const table of tables) {\n        const result = await db.query<{ relrowsecurity: boolean }>(\n          `SELECT c.relrowsecurity\n           FROM pg_class c\n           JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace\n           WHERE n.nspname = $1 AND c.relname = $2 AND c.relkind = 'r'`,\n          [tenantSchemaName, table],","numLines":75,"startLine":300,"totalLines":505}}}

{ InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\nimport { Repository, FindOptionsWhere, MoreThanOrEqual, LessThanOrEqual } from 'typeorm';\nimport { QueryHandler, IQueryHandler } from @InjectRepository(Payroll)\n    private readonly payrollRepository: Repository<Payroll>,\n  ) {}\n\n  async execute(query: GetPayrollsQuery): Promise<PaginatedQueryResult<Payroll>> {\n    const { Why it matters: this pair was about to become repository ground truth, which suppresses the finding class, quarantines the rule and scores the judges — so a third voice that merely echoes adds no information while multiplying the consequence. What breaks if it is skipped: a wrong suppression blinds test-gap detection for every future migration in this repository, and a wrong confirmation pushes authors toward mirror-copy specs that drift from the code they shadow. The downstream surface is feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus, which folds this verdict into the rule registry for test-gap-adapter. What proves the result is the chain above, each link read at file:line in this run: the migration declares a catalog-reading postcondition, two runners invoke it and roll back on failure, CI applies the migration on any pull request touching a migrations path, and an invariant spec locks the registry entry that makes all of it reachable.\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Verdict: `false_positive`, confidence 0.88 — the prior verdicts survive, but not as I received them.**\n\nThe three things worth your attention:\n\n- **The chain that decides this isn't in either prior rationale.** Both judges argued about the *negative* — whether the search supporting \"no test exists\" was adequate. I argued the *affirmative*: this migration is verified by a postcondition that reads PostgreSQL's catalog (`1802200000000-DropPayrollCurrencyDefault.ts:37`), invoked by two runners that roll back on failure (`migration-orchestrator.ts:291,302`), executed in CI on every PR touching a migrations path (`db-migration-check.yml:91,424`).\n- **I tested the one thing that would have flipped it.** If this migration weren't registered in a runner, the entire chain would be vacuous and the finding a true positive. `critical-infra-ssot.spec.ts:264` asserts db-migrate's registry carries the hr migrations glob, and that assertion is itself a spec — so registration can't silently lapse.\n- **One refutation landed partially.** `down()` at line 50 restores `SET DEFAULT 'USD'` and no gate I can cite verifies it — `finance-currency-ssot.spec.ts` scans only `*.handler.ts`/`*.entity.ts`, never migrations. I judged it a thin residue rather than the gap the rule alleges, but it's recorded in `residual_uncertainty` rather than buried.\n\nTwo caveats on my own work: I had no Bash on this route, so the \"no importing spec\" premise still rests partly on the prior judges' greps — though that uncertainty points toward false positive, not away. And note the arithmetic: counting all three judges, mean confidence is 0.767, below the 0.80 bar, held down by the adversarial judge's 0.62.
